# QA baseline — test.gokesari.com, 27-Nov-2026 (Phase 0)

Baseline of the existing automated QA suite (`tests/qa`) on staging before the large build. This list is what must still pass at the end: every check marked **pass** here must still pass, and each **fail** below is a known failure at baseline (not caused by the build).

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Site | https://test.gokesari.com (staging only; nothing was run against gokesari.com) |
| Commit served (`/sw.js` VERSION) | `79e578732ccfa3b662a9cf0a27aefcfd87348d22` (= `origin/staging` head at run start) |
| Run start | 27-Nov-2026 01.01 IST |
| Run end | 27-Nov-2026 02.27 IST |
| Suite | `tests/qa/guest`, `tests/qa/signed-in`, `tests/qa/scenarios` — `npx playwright test -c playwright.qa.config.ts`, projects desktop + mobile (Pixel 7), 1 worker |
| Sessions | Saved staging sessions in `.e2e-auth/` were all still signed in (customer, customer2, shop, shop-main, rider, admin, admin2, ops, oplim); no new sign-in codes were requested. `shopB` and `rider2` have no saved session (Gmail connector not authorised in this session), so their checks are **blocked**. |
| Test code | Not modified. |
| Raw results | `test-results/qa/test.gokesari.com/{desktop,mobile}.jsonl`, `test-results/qa/playwright-report-test.gokesari.com.json`, traces/screenshots under `test-results/qa/artifacts/test.gokesari.com/` (local, git-ignored) |

## Totals

| Count by | pass | fail | blocked | skipped | total |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Checks (one Playwright test × project) | 497 | 108 | 668 | 60 | 1333 |
| Case records (one row per TC/SC ID × project) | 581 | 119 | 745 | 60 | 1505 |
| Distinct case IDs (worst result over checks and projects) | 403 | 90 | 653 | 57 | 1203 |

Playwright's own summary: 497 passed, 108 failed, 1767 skipped (Playwright counts *blocked* and tier-skipped checks, and mobile copies of desktop-only checks, as skipped).

- **Guest tier: no failures** (every guest check passed or was blocked/not automated).
- **blocked** = a precondition is missing on staging or the case is declared manual/not automated (304 of the case records are "not automated: added … for manual testing"); see each row's note.
- **skipped** = local-tier checks (need the local database / secret), recorded as skipped on staging by rule.

Per-area summary (checks):

| Area | pass | fail | blocked | skipped |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sign-in, accounts and security | 43 | 10 | 16 | 14 |
| Browse and search (storefront, discovery, location) | 185 | 17 | 156 | 0 |
| Cart and order (checkout, order states, fulfilment, delivery, returns) | 33 | 27 | 102 | 0 |
| Shop registration (/sell) and seller tools | 65 | 19 | 135 | 0 |
| Franchise and dealer (/franchise, /franchise/book, dealer platform) | 63 | 8 | 105 | 0 |
| Delivery partner (apply, KYC, payouts) | 1 | 4 | 1 | 4 |
| Admin and operator screens (back office, staff roles, finance, KPIs) | 15 | 15 | 83 | 0 |
| Wallet and payments (sandbox only) | 21 | 5 | 21 | 0 |
| End-to-end scenarios | 0 | 0 | 2 | 41 |
| Other areas (field validation, regression, societies, misc.) | 71 | 3 | 47 | 1 |

Observed patterns behind the failures (not yet triaged against the code or decision log; to be confirmed before logging defects):

- Staff accounts (support / finance / oplim) now get "Please verify both your mobile number and your e-mail to continue" (403 or redirect to `/account/verify`) — the identity-verification gate now applies to these staff test accounts (TC-STAFF-002/003/047/063/065, TC-PUJA-049).
- Shop-order UI: the "Accept order / Reject / Start picking / Offer substitute" buttons are not found on the shop's order card (TC-FUL-001…013, TC-RGR-012), and the cart pay button ("Pay from wallet|Place order") is not found (TC-UX-002, TC-CART-011, TC-LOC-022) — UI changed or test-shop state.
- Test data on staging: shop A "Asmy test… is not taking new orders right now", "Butter Cookies … unavailable online", COD radio disabled (customer has 2 COD orders on the way), stock counts off by one after cancel (TC-ORD-006/010/011/012), dealer schemes already archived / extra published versions (TC-DPLT-002/031).
- Admin four-eye rule: setting `fulfilmentOptions` now returns 202 "Sent for approval" (TC-DEL-020).
- Seller Central menus/labels ("Update many products with Excel", "How do you deliver?", "Change price or offer by voice", seller-topbar status pill, inventory headers in Marathi).

## Notes on the requested areas

- **Delivery partner apply**: the suite has no guest check for `/delivery-partner/apply`; covered only by SC-GW (fail), TC-RGR-010 (fail: heading "My Delivery Partner Application" not found) and TC-UX-003 (pass: `/delivery-partner` loads with no console errors). Supplementary read-only GET outside the suite at the end of the run: `/delivery-partner/apply` 200, `/delivery-partner/register` 307 → `/signin`, `/sell` 200, `/franchise` 200, `/franchise/book` 200.
- **Payments**: sandbox only. TC-PAY-036 "Pay online opens this site's test gateway" passed; the built-in sandbox page check, UPI on gateway (TC-PAY-017), registration-fee sandbox (SD-03) and renewal link (TC-REGSUB-018) were blocked (preconditions), so no sandbox payment was completed in this run.
- **Shop registration (/sell)**: identity-first and registration-brief checks are partly blocked ("this account's registration is past the identity step", need a new test account / owner-agreed test PAN-GST).

## Triage (27-Nov-2026 03.29 IST)

Every one of the 108 failing checks above was traced to ONE cause against the code on `origin/staging` and
docs/decision-log.md, fixed in the suite where the cause was the test or its world, and re-run on test.gokesari.com
only (the signed-in specs that failed; the guest tier had no failures; nothing against gokesari.com). Branch `fix/qa-baseline-2026-11-27` (worktree
`/Users/agtci/GoKesari-qa-fix`), not pushed.

Cause legend: **(a)** test drift — the product changed on purpose; **(b)** staging data / environment drift — fixed
through the suite's own world set-up where possible, otherwise blocked with the exact reset; **(c)** product defect —
left failing and logged in `docs/testing/qa-defects.json`.

| Count by cause | rows (checks) | re-run result |
|---|---|---|
| (a) test drift | 44 | pass 34, blocked 10 |
| (b) staging data / environment drift | 61 | pass 51, blocked 10 |
| (c) product defect | 3 | fail 3 |
| **Total** | **108** | **pass 85, fail 3, blocked 20** |

Before → after for these 108 checks: **108 fail → 85 pass, 3 fail, 20 blocked.**

### Re-runs (test.gokesari.com, serving `5b91743d`)

| Run | When (IST) | What | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| Re-run 1 (interrupted) | 27-Nov-2026, ended 02.56 | the 29 failing specs, failing IDs only, after the two common fixes | desktop part only; used for triage, not counted |
| Re-run 2 | 27-Nov-2026 02.57–03.15 | the 29 failing specs, failing IDs (+ TC-CART-009), desktop + mobile | 102 pass, 17 fail, 25 blocked case records |
| Re-run 3 | 27-Nov-2026 03.17–03.26 | whole files order-lifecycle, shopping-cart, p0-staff-rbac-v2 (chained checks the filtered run cut), then TC-RBAC-004 and TC-MKT-019 | 47 pass, 5 fail, 20 blocked case records (the 5 fails: TC-RBAC-004 WhatsApp routes, TC-MKT-019 /r/ link under v2, TC-CART-012 v2 pay step — fixed, see re-run 4) |
| Re-run 4 | 27-Nov-2026 03.27–03.29 | TC-RBAC-004, TC-MKT-019, TC-CART-012, TC-CART-007, desktop + mobile | 6 pass, 0 fail (4 mobile copies not applicable) |

The per-row result below is the latest run of that check (re-run 4, else 3, else 2). Raw results were
kept outside the repository (`test-results/qa/test.gokesari.com/` of the worktree; git-ignored).

### What caused most rows

1. **Seller Central in Marathi (40 rows, b).** Module 6 saves the EN / हिं / मरा choice on the account
   (`users.preferred_lang`, src/server/board-lang.ts). TC-UX-009 switches the shop owner to मरा and never switched
   back, so every later shop-owner page (orders, inventory, finance, product set-up, voice price, Seller Central frame)
   opened in Marathi and the English locators failed. Fix: every suite session starts with `gk_lang=en`
   (tests/qa/lib/actors.ts) and TC-UX-009 clicks English again in its `finally` (the account is English again).
2. **dual_auth for the staff / rider test accounts (14 rows, a).** dual_auth is ON on staging (migration 0219, PR
   #637). gk-oplim and gk-admin2 had no mobile, the rider an unverified one, so their APIs answered 403 "Please verify
   both your mobile number and your e-mail to continue." and staff pages redirected to /account/verify. dual_auth was
   left ON; the suite now completes the missing factor exactly as /account/verify does (tests/qa/lib/dual-auth.ts:
   `POST /api/dual-auth/verify` mobile-code → code read from the MOCK SMS outbox with the admin session →
   mobile-verify; a number-less account gets the suite's fixed test mobile 99999 + 5 digits of its id, the same one
   the checkout SMS step uses). Done once per account per worker, never on production. The e-mail factor was already
   verified on every account (emailed sign-in code). Sensitive actions (contact / bank change) still ask for BOTH codes
   (step-up): those checks assert the "Confirm it's you" dialog and are **blocked**, because the e-mailed code needs the
   inbox (Gmail not authorised in this session).
3. **Onboarding v2 / guest cart / referral sharing / dealer schemes D1–D10 / Rider App v2 (a).** Intended product
   changes; checks updated (several were already updated on origin/staging by cef9c023 after the baseline's test code).
4. **E2E bakery stock (b).** Butter Cookies (world "packaged") sold out; the fallback picked the cheapest listing,
   a newspaper that does not track stock, so stock and substitute checks read the wrong numbers. The shop owner now
   restocks the preset (E-33's suggestion) — and the fallback exposed **D-172** (below).
5. **Night run (b).** The baseline ran 01.01–02.27 IST: shop A's 06:00–22:00 hours made it closed and no rider offer
   can come before the 07.00 IST delivery slot. "Open now" / closed-shop checks now set no hours for their step; the
   rider-offer steps stay **blocked** at night and need a run between 07.00 and 22.00 IST.

| D-172 (new) | TC-ORD-006, 010, 011, TC-FUL-003 (seen through the untracked fallback listing) | cancelling an order restocks a listing with trackInventory = false: "The Times of India" grew from ~186 to 265 over the runs although checkout never takes its stock | src/server/services/orders.ts:1476-1499 (cancel → restockOnline for every line, no trackInventory filter; compare src/server/payments/order-payments.ts:120 and fulfilment.ts trackedUnits) |

Not logged as defects: the Seller Central registration page shows two "Registration date" values (9 Oct on the Shop
Registration card = start of the paid period, `registration_start_date`; 8 Oct under Registration details = the shop's
registration) — by design of PR #557 but easy to misread (owner may want a different label); "A archived scheme
cannot become archived." uses "A" before a vowel (copy).

### Staging data resets still needed (not done here: database or owner/admin actions)

1. **Business Partner dealer scheme is ARCHIVED** (owner's D2 says SUSPENDED). An earlier run of TC-DPLT-031 sent its
   "archive must be refused" request to the then-SUSPENDED scheme, which accepted it (suspended → archived is allowed;
   archived is final). Reset: `UPDATE dealer_schemes SET status = 'SUSPENDED' WHERE code = 'BUSINESS_PARTNER'` on
   `gokesari_test` (owner's OK). TC-DPLT-001 stays blocked until then; the check no longer sends that request.
2. **Customer gk-lc14 has 2 open cash-on-delivery orders** older than the newest 25 the API lists; COD is disabled
   for the account until an operator cancels or delivers them (TC-COD-001/002/012 blocked).
3. **shopMembership.graceDays is stored as 15** on staging (saved before the 7-day default of 25-Nov-2026): Admin →
   Settings → shopMembership → 7 or "Use default" (needs the second admin's approval). TC-REGSUB-004 blocked.
4. Done in this session: the 4 pending "Change setting fulfilmentOptions" approval requests that TC-DEL-020 raised in
   the baseline and re-run 1 were **withdrawn by the admin who raised them** (nothing approved or rejected); the
   suite now withdraws its own request and blocks the case (rule changes are local-tier on staging), as it did in
   re-run 2. Three TC-STAFF-061 shop change proposals left pending by the interrupted / filtered re-runs were withdrawn
   by their proposer (gk-oplim). Still pending and not from this session: 8 "Reactivate account" and 2 journal-entry
   requests (JE-000025, JE-000027 from 23-Nov). The shop
   owner's saved language is English again. gk-oplim (…4179), the rider (…0105) and, when used, gk-admin2
   now have a verified mobile through the mock SMS outbox.

### Still blocked (not failed) and why

- shopB and rider2 have no saved session (Gmail not authorised): their checks are blocked, not failed (e.g. TC-RGR-010 needs rider2 once the rider has no check outstanding).
- E-mailed codes (dual_auth step-up for mobile / e-mail / bank changes): TC-AUTH-024, TC-AUTH-026, TC-RPAY-001.
- Night: rider offers (SC-GW, TC-FUL-005/006, TC-ORD-010 and the order-lifecycle delivery chain) — re-run between 07.00 and 22.00 IST.
- Staging data above: TC-DPLT-001, TC-COD-001/002/012, TC-REGSUB-004; TC-STAFF-047 needs unclaimed PAID payout records; TC-RGR-009 needs a shop record that is not verified.
